Conclusion: 该综述全面概述了基于移动图像的医疗解决方案及其在低成本疾病诊断与监测方面的潜力，指出了已有进展、尚需深入研究的方向，以及应用特定和共性的挑战。

Abstract: Advances in processing power, camera technologies, and mobile image analysis have made smartphones and other mobile devices, such as laptops, increasingly suitable for medical diagnosis and healthcare applications. Researchers have developed low-cost solutions for the early detection and monitoring of various health conditions, including eye and ENT diseases, malnutrition, heart rate variability, skin and oral conditions, and injuries, using images captured by non-medical devices such as smartphones and webcams. This survey examines existing research on mobile image-based medical diagnosis, with an emphasis on its potential to enable low-cost and accessible healthcare. We comparatively analyze state-of-the-art solutions across different healthcare application categories, examining their advantages and limitations. Based on this analysis, we identify desirable characteristics of mobile image-based diagnostic tools and highlight areas where existing approaches have made progress as well as areas requiring further research. We also discuss application-specific and common challenges and outline directions for future research. Overall, this study provides a comprehensive overview of mobile image-based healthcare solutions and their potential to support low-cost disease diagnosis and monitoring, particularly for underserved populations in remote and resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [210] [ZVeC: A Zero-Shot Framework for Instance-Level Vehicle Extraction and Generative Point Cloud Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24825)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Boris Jutzi,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZVeC提出一种零样本、实例驱动的框架，将场景级点云补全重构为组合式的对象级重建，在无大规模监督数据的地下场景中实现了几何一致的补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境中采集的LiDAR点云因遮挡和有限视角存在严重几何不完整，而缺乏大规模监督数据集使得可靠的点云补全十分困难，因此需要一种无需场景特定训练的方法。

Method: 将场景分解为语义对象实例以降低杂乱环境中的重建歧义；对每个分割出的车辆实例，使用深度与3D高斯条件化的扩散模型利用泛化几何先验独立补全，再将重建实例重组回原场景；并构建了真实世界地下停车场的稠密LiDAR基准。

Result: 在定量指标和视觉质量上均持续优于代表性场景级基线；补全点云与实测输入差异显著（平均KL散度约2.1），但将输入降至原始LiDAR测量的1%时补全结果变化很小（KL散度<0.50）。

Conclusion: ZVeC即使在高稀疏输入下也能产生几何一致的补全，验证了零样本实例驱动框架的有效性。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds acquired in underground environments exhibit severe geometric incompleteness due to occlusions and limited sensor viewpoints, making reliable point cloud completion challenging without large supervised datasets. We propose ZVeC, a zero-shot, instance-driven framework that reformulates scene-level completion as compositional object-level reconstruction. By decomposing a scene into semantic object instances, ZVeC reduces reconstruction ambiguity in cluttered environments while eliminating the need for scenario-specific training. Each segmented vehicle is completed independently using a depth- and 3D Gaussian-conditioned diffusion model that exploits generalized geometric priors before the reconstructed instances are recomposed into the original scene. To evaluate our approach, we construct a real-world dense LiDAR benchmark of underground parking environments. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements over representative scene-level baselines in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. The completed point cloud differs substantially from the measured input (average KL divergence ~ 2.1), yet reducing the input to only 1% of the original LiDAR measurements changes the completed reconstruction only marginally (KL divergence < 0.50). This demonstrates that ZVeC produces geometrically consistent completions even under extreme input sparsity.

</details>


### [211] [When Wider Views Fail: Stress-Testing Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24839)
*Daisy Li,Kyle Gao,Quanyun Wu,Hanna Chomko,John S. Zelek,Jonathan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究前馈式三维重建模型在视角变化这一分布偏移下的失效模式，发现视角跨度增大时模型性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式三维重建模型能从稀疏图像高效估计几何结构，但其预训练特性使其在面对训练数据之外的分布偏移时十分脆弱。识别这些失效模式对于判断模型能否在无约束成像环境中可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 将视角变化作为受控的分布偏移，在保持输入图像数量固定的前提下改变稀疏图像输入的角度跨度，并在多个前馈式重建模型上进行测试。

Result: 随着视角跨度增大，多个模型的性能均出现显著退化；大跨度下不仅产生不完整的表面覆盖，还生成缺乏观测图像支撑的几何结构。

Conclusion: 视角变化会引发超出传统重建不完整性之外的失效模式，说明需要在挑战模型所学几何先验的分布偏移条件下评估预训练前馈模型。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models enable efficient geometry estimation from sparse images, but their pretrained nature can make them vulnerable to distribution shifts beyond their training data. Identifying these failure modes is important for understanding when such models can be reliably deployed in unconstrained imaging settings. We investigate viewpoint variation as a controlled distribution shift by varying the angular span of sparse image inputs while keeping the input budget fixed. Across multiple feed-forward reconstruction models, we observe substantial degradation as viewpoint span increases, with wide spans producing both incomplete surface coverage and geometry unsupported by the observed imagery. These results reveal that viewpoint variation can induce failure modes beyond conventional reconstruction incompleteness, highlighting the need to evaluate pretrained feed-forward models under distribution shifts that challenge their learned geometric priors.

</details>


### [212] [Revisiting Multi-View Stereo: A Sequence-to-Sequence Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24850)
*Aoxiang Fan,Corentin Dumery,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究已知相机参数下的多视图立体（MVS）问题，提出融合传统MVS与前馈（FF）方法的新范式：将MVS从"序列到单点"（仅预测单参考视图深度）重构为"序列到序列"（联合预测所有输入视图几何），并设计基于全局Transformer、显式利用相机先验的架构，在多个公开基准上取得超越MVS与FF基线的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多视图图像中计算精确几何是计算机视觉的基础问题。近期前馈模型虽能联合估计3D几何与相机参数，但即使给定真实相机参数，仍受重建歧义导致的几何失真困扰。作者希望弥合传统MVS与前馈方法之间的差距，解决几何失真问题。

Method: 提出新颖方法：1）将MVS从仅对单一参考视图预测深度的"序列到单点"映射，重构为类似FF模型的"序列到序列"任务，联合预测所有输入视图的几何；2）引入全局Transformer架构，包含两个显式利用相机先验的组件——将相机参数注入图像块token的射线图嵌入（使Transformer具备相机感知能力），以及替代传统逐视图代价体积的统一全局代价体积，用以跨所有视图联合捕获3D结构。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，该方法达到最先进（state-of-the-art）性能，超越了MVS与前馈重建两类基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过在已知相机参数下将MVS重构为序列到序列任务，并借助显式利用相机先验的全局Transformer架构，本方法有效融合了传统MVS与前馈方法的优势，缓解了几何失真问题，在多个基准上验证了其优越性与有效性。

Abstract: Computing accurate geometry from multi-view images is a fundamental problem in computer vision. Recent feed-forward (FF) models jointly estimate 3D geometry and camera parameters, but they typically suffer from geometry distortion caused by reconstruction ambiguity, even when ground-truth camera parameters are supplied. In this paper, we study the multi-view stereo (MVS) problem with known camera parameters and propose a novel approach that bridges conventional MVS and FF methods. Rather than casting MVS as a sequence-to-one mapping that predicts depth only for a single reference view, we reformulate it as a sequence-to-sequence task, akin to FF models, that jointly predicts geometry for all input views. We introduce a global transformer-based architecture with two components that explicitly exploit camera-induced priors: ray-map embeddings that inject camera parameters into image patch tokens, making the transformer camera-aware, and a unified global cost volume that replaces conventional per-view cost volumes to jointly capture 3D structure across all views. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing both MVS and FF reconstruction baselines.

</details>


### [213] [DTKDP: A Dual Teacher Knowledge Distillation and Pruning Framework for Lightweight Oriented SAR Ship Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24872)
*Yuming Li,Fan Zhang,Alin M. Achim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DTKDP框架，通过双教师知识蒸馏与剪枝实现轻量化旋转SAR舰船检测，在精度接近原模型的同时大幅压缩参数与计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段旋转检测器在SAR舰船检测中定位精度高，但其大骨干网络、特征金字塔、候选框模块和沉重的RoI头难以部署；现有轻量级SAR舰船检测器多为单阶段框架，缺乏候选框级细化，难以实现精确旋转定位。

Method: DTKDP在卷积、归一化和线性层中引入可学习门控，以剪枝卷积通道和RoI头神经元；提出旋转候选框对齐（RPA），在教师生成的共享旋转候选框空间中蒸馏师生预测；并采用双教师方案，由同构主教师提供分类与回归指导，异构辅助教师提供补充分类线索。

Result: 在SSDD和RSDD-SAR数据集上，DTKDP将ResNet-50骨干的Oriented R-CNN与RoI Transformer参数量减少87.5-91.8%，FLOPs减少75.6-79.9%；AP50、AP75、mAP50:75、mAP50:95相对变化从下降2.38%到提升0.65%；相比RTMDet-tiny在四个指标上提升0.52-27.55%，并持续优于代表性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: DTKDP在保持接近全尺寸模型精度的同时显著降低参数量与计算量，展现出良好的精度-效率权衡，适用于轻量化旋转SAR舰船检测部署。

Abstract: Two-stage oriented detectors achieve high localization accuracy in synthetic aperture radar (SAR) ship detection, but their large backbones, feature pyramids, proposal modules, and heavy region of interest (RoI) heads hinder deployment. Existing lightweight SAR ship detectors typically use one-stage frameworks that lack proposal-level refinement for precise rotated localization. This paper presents a dual-teacher knowledge distillation and pruning (DTKDP) framework for lightweight oriented SAR ship detection. DTKDP introduces learnable gates into convolutional, normalization, and linear layers to prune convolutional channels and RoI-head neurons. Rotated proposal alignment (RPA) distills teacher and student predictions in a shared teacher-generated rotated proposal space, while a dual-teacher scheme combines classification and regression guidance from a homogeneous main teacher with complementary classification cues from a heterogeneous auxiliary teacher. Experiments on the SAR Ship Detection Dataset (SSDD) and Rotated Ship Detection Dataset in SAR Images (RSDD-SAR) show that DTKDP reduces the parameters of Oriented Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Oriented R-CNN) and RoI Transformer equipped with ResNet-50 backbones by 87.5-91.8% and their floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 75.6-79.9%. In terms of average precision (AP) and mean average precision (mAP), the resulting Oriented R-CNN-slim and RoI Transformer-slim retain accuracy close to their full-scale counterparts. Relative changes across $\mathrm{AP}_{50}$, $\mathrm{AP}_{75}$, $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:75}$, and $\mathrm{mAP}_{50:95}$ range from a 2.38% decrease to a 0.65% improvement. Compared with RTMDet-tiny, they improve all four metrics on both datasets by 0.52-27.55% and consistently surpass representative distillation methods, demonstrating a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [214] [SPHQuant: Efficient extreme low bit weight quantization for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24875)
*Kewei Zhang,Zheng Chen,Haotong Qin,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPHQuant：一种用于VLM的无旋转球面权重仅量化框架，在极低位宽下匹配SOTA精度，解码吞吐较QTIP提升30.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生多模态VLM成为下一代基础模型核心，但其大语言主干内存占用高、自回归解码受内存限制，难以边缘部署。权重仅训练后量化是实用方案，但极低位宽仍困难：无旋转方法在2-3比特受离群值影响，旋转方法精度提升但带来额外运行时开销。

Method: 提出无旋转的球面权重仅量化框架。将每个8D权向量分解为坐标符号、半径与正单位方向，把离群幅度隔离到半径中，使方向有界且统计规整；据此给半径分配额外精度以缓解离群导致的精度下降。使用紧凑的正方向码本，并通过角度参数化微调码本项以保持单位球约束。设计硬件友好的GEMV内核，使方向码本小到可放入共享内存查找，并高效打包径向比特。

Result: 实验表明SPHQuant在极低位量化下性能与SOTA方法相当，在RTX A6000上解码吞吐较QTIP提升30.3%。

Conclusion: SPHQuant通过球面分解与码本设计，在无旋转开销下实现极低位VLM量化，兼顾精度与解码效率，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent foundation models are moving toward native multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs), making VLMs a central form of next-generation foundation models. However, their large language backbones make edge deployment difficult due to high memory footprint and memory-bound autoregressive decoding. Weight-only post-training quantization is a practical solution, but pushing VLMs to extreme low bit-widths remains challenging: existing rotation-free methods suffer from outliers at 2-3 bits, while rotation-based methods improve accuracy at the cost of additional runtime overhead. We propose SPHQuant, a rotation-free spherical weight-only quantization framework for VLMs. Instead of quantizing weights directly in Cartesian coordinates, SPHQuant decomposes each 8D weight vector into coordinate signs, radius, and a positive unit direction. This representation isolates outlier magnitude into the radius while keeping directions bounded and statistically regular. Based on this insight, SPHQuant allocates extra precision to the radius to mitigate accuracy degradation induced by outliers. It further uses a compact positive-direction codebook and fine-tunes codebook entries through angular parameterization to preserve the unit-sphere constraint. We also design a hardware-friendly GEMV kernel that keeps the direction codebook small enough for shared-memory lookup and packs radial bits efficiently. Experiments show that SPHQuant matches the performance of state-of-the-art extreme low-bit quantization methods while improving decode throughput over QTIP by 30.3% on RTX A6000. Code will be released in https://github.com/Pushazf/SPHQuant.

</details>


### [215] [Generating Chest X-Ray Counterfactuals by Specialising Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24879)
*Xiaodan Xing,Rajat R. Rasal,Julia A. Meister,Sara Ghorayeb,Galvin Khara,Jessica Schrouff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 我们提出 specialisation 框架，将预训练的非因果生成模型在分布偏移下高效适配为因果机制，据此训练的 RadCF 模型在胸部 X 光反事实生成中提升了因果合理性、数据与参数效率，并能检测和缓解下游分类器的捷径学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实图像生成方法在感知质量、身份保持和因果模型忠实度上已有进展，但在医疗场景应用受限：标注数据稀缺、数据集间存在分布偏移、预训练生成模型与反事实推理所需模型不匹配。

Method: 提出 specialisation——一种数据与参数高效框架，可将预训练的非因果生成模型在分布偏移下适配为因果机制；基于该框架，用潜空间流匹配（latent flow matching）训练放射学反事实图像生成模型 RadCF。在三个胸部 X 光数据集上验证，涵盖不同数据集偏移、数据量及反事实问题，并涉及高度局部化的干预。代码已开源。

Result: RadCF 与 specialisation 相比现有方法在反事实合理性上更优，且具有数据与参数效率；生成的反事实可用于检测并缓解下游医学分类器中的捷径学习。

Conclusion: specialisation 框架有效解决了医疗场景下反事实图像生成面临的分布偏移与模型不匹配问题，RadCF 在胸部 X 光数据上验证了其有效性与效率，并展示了对下游任务（捷径学习检测与缓解）的实际价值。

Abstract: Counterfactual image generation answers questions about how a subject would have looked under retrospective, hypothetical scenarios. Recent methods have improved perceptual quality, identity preservation and faithfulness to an underlying causal model, but their adoption in healthcare is limited by scarce annotated data, distribution shift between datasets, and mismatches between pretrained generative models and those required for counterfactual inference. We propose specialisation, a data and parameter-efficient framework for adapting pretrained, non-causal generative models into causal mechanisms under distribution shift. Based on this framework, we train a radiology counterfactual image generation model, called RadCF, using latent flow matching. We validate our approach on three chest X-ray datasets spanning different dataset shifts, data volumes, and counterfactual questions, associated with challenging, highly-localised interventions. Our results show that RadCF and specialisation improve counterfactual soundness over existing methods while being data and parameter efficient, and that the resulting counterfactuals can detect and mitigate shortcut learning in a downstream medical classifier. Code is available at https://github.com/GSK-AI/RadCF/.

</details>


### [216] [PixelDiT2: Representation-Grounded Pixel Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24919)
*Yongsheng Yu,Wei Xiong,Yichen Sheng,Shiqiu Liu,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixelDiT2，通过表征接地策略为像素空间扩散模型引入显式表征先验，在无自编码器的情况下加速收敛并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型虽在图像质量上接近潜空间扩散，但收敛更慢、最终质量仍有差距，核心原因在于缺少显式表征先验：潜空间扩散在紧凑结构化的潜空间中去噪，而像素扩散需同时从原始RGB空间学习适合去噪的表征与像素生成。

Method: 提出端到端像素空间扩散模型PixelDiT2，在不引入自编码器或潜重建瓶颈的前提下，将表征学习与像素生成解耦；具体采用"表征接地"策略，用冻结的预训练视觉基础模型在整个去噪过程中为每个图像块提供显式表征引导，使像素扩散Transformer更专注于像素生成。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上，训练600轮后FID达到1.46；在512x512分辨率下，训练680轮后FID达到1.48。

Conclusion: 显式表征先验可有效缓解像素空间扩散收敛慢、质量滞后的问题，PixelDiT2在无自编码器条件下取得了有竞争力的生成性能。

Abstract: Recent advances in pixel-space diffusion models have narrowed the image quality gap with latent-space diffusion, but still converge more slowly and lag behind in final image quality. We argue that a key reason is the lack of an explicit representation prior: unlike latent diffusion, which usually denoises in a compact and structured latent space, pixel diffusion needs to learn denoising-friendly representations and pixel generation simultaneously from raw RGB space. To address this problem, we propose PixelDiT2, an end-to-end pixel-space diffusion model designed to decouple representation learning from pixel generation without introducing an autoencoder or latent reconstruction bottleneck. We propose representation grounding that uses a frozen pretrained vision foundation model to provide explicit per-patch representation guidance throughout denoising, allowing the pixel diffusion transformer to focus more on pixel generation. On ImageNet-256x256, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.46 after 600 epochs; at 512x512 resolution, PixelDiT2 achieves an FID of 1.48 after 680 epochs.

</details>


### [217] [Anatomy-Decomposed Chest Computed Tomography (CT) Projections as Scalable Supervision for Bone Suppression in Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24937)
*Mrunmay Angaitkar,Piyush Kumar,Aarjav Satia,Pranav Rao,Ashish Mittal,Manoj Tadepalli,Preetham Putha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于CT数字重建影像（DRR）的框架，用合成配对监督实现胸片骨骼/肺成分抑制，并在多数据集检测任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片中骨骼重叠会遮挡异常，而配对训练数据稀缺，限制了监督式骨骼抑制方法的训练。

Method: 提出DRR框架：用新的骨骼分割算法将CT分解为骨骼、非肺软组织与肺三部分并分别投影，加权组合得到像素配准、分量之和恰等于完整DRR的合成胸片；模型通过预测目标成分并用减法恢复剩余部分来抑制骨骼或肺成分；进一步在真实胸片上输出目标域，进行无配对的成分级DRR翻译。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，下游检测实验显示骨骼抑制有效，增益集中于骨骼重叠明显的异常；与开源DRR引擎相比，未修改DRR在真实感与标签相关解剖保留上相当，翻译后DRR取得最佳FID、肺野锐度及与源CT解剖的一致性。

Conclusion: DRR合成配对监督可在无真实配对数据下实现成分抑制并迁移到真实胸片，成分级DRR翻译进一步缩小外观差距，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Bone overlap can obscure abnormalities in chest radiographs, while scarce paired training data limit supervised bone suppression. We address this challenge with a digitally reconstructed radiograph (DRR) framework that converts chest computed tomography (CT) into paired supervision for component suppression. A novel bone segmentation algorithm enables CT decomposition into bone, non-lung soft-tissue, and lung components, which are projected separately. Their weighted combination yields synthetic radiographs with pixel-registered component images that sum exactly to the full DRR. Models trained on these data suppress bone or lung components by predicting the target component and recovering the remainder by subtraction, transferring to real radiographs without real paired training data. As an extension, their outputs on real radiographs provide target domains for unpaired, component-wise DRR translation, reducing the appearance gap while retaining anatomical details. Across multiple public datasets, downstream detection experiments demonstrate the utility of bone suppression, with gains concentrated on abnormalities with substantial bone overlap. Compared with open-source DRR engines applied to the same CTs, our unmodified DRRs achieve comparable realism and preservation of label-relevant anatomy, while translated DRRs achieve the best Fréchet inception distance (FID), lung-field sharpness, and agreement with source-CT anatomy among the evaluated methods. Models and inference code: https://huggingface.co/qureaiorg/bone-suppression; Translated projections: https://huggingface.co/datasets/qureaiorg/ct2xr-projections.

</details>


### [218] [GAE: Learning a Geometry-Native Latent Space for 3D-Consistent World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24981)
*Jiahao Lu,Minghao Yin,Wenbo Hu,Hengyu Liu,Wang Zhao,Sai-Kit Yeung,Ying Shan,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将几何基础模型特征重参数化为紧凑的几何原生隐空间（GAE），作为感知与生成的共享接口，显著提升生成质量与3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器能产生逼真画面，却难以维持一致的3D场景；作者认为这不仅是建模问题，更是表示问题——生成器通常演化以外观为中心的隐变量，而感知模型在语义丰富的空间中恢复几何结构。因此，与其把几何作为额外输出，不如改变潜在表示本身。

Method: 提出几何原生自编码器（GAE），将几何基础模型的特征重参数化为紧凑隐空间；该隐空间可联合解码为外观、深度、相机参数与点图（point maps）。基于此表示，使用标准条件流（conditional flow）即可支持多种生成任务。

Result: 在固定生成器与训练协议的受控对比中，以GAE替换原有隐变量同时提升了视觉质量与独立测量的3D一致性：在RealEstate10K和DL3DV上FVD分别下降12.7%和23.1%，RealEstate10K上相机轨迹误差减半。

Conclusion: 隐空间是几何一致生成的核心，几何原生隐空间可作为感知与生成之间的共享接口。

Abstract: We present a compact geometry-native latent space as a shared foundation for perception and generation. Visual generators can produce photorealistic frames without preserving a consistent 3D scene. We argue that this is not only a modeling problem but also a representation problem: generators typically evolve appearance-centric latents, while perception models recover geometry in a semantically rich space that encodes cross-view structure. Rather than adding geometry as another output, we reparameterize a geometry foundation model's features into a compact latent space for generation. We realize this shift with the geometry-native autoencoder (GAE), whose latent is jointly decodable to appearance, depth, cameras, and point maps. With this state, a standard conditional flow supports diverse generation tasks. In controlled comparisons that hold the generator and training protocol fixed, replacing the latent with GAE improves both visual quality and independently measured 3D coherence: FVD falls by $12.7\%$ and $23.1\%$ on RealEstate10K and DL3DV, and camera-trajectory error is halved on RealEstate10K. Together, these results show that the latent space is central to geometry-consistent generation and can serve as a shared interface between perception and generation.

</details>


### [219] [WorldCrafter: Consistent Video World Model with Implicit 3D-aware Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24984)
*Wangbo Yu,Kunhao Liu,Wenbo Hu,Shenghai Yuan,Chaoran Feng,Haiyang Zhou,Yukun Huang,Yiran Wang,Wang Zhao,Yingmin Luo,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldCrafter是一个视频世界模型，通过相机可查询的隐式3D感知记忆，实现长时间跨视角的场景探索，显著提升长时一致性与相机控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽能进行动态环境的交互式探索，但在长时间跨度及不同视角下难以保持对先前观测的一致性。需要一种能有效保留历史观测信息的方法。

Method: 提出WorldCrafter，学习相机可查询的隐式3D感知记忆。核心思路是让请求视角决定多视角证据如何压缩进视频生成器有限的token预算。记忆编码器与位姿条件读出模块与视频生成器联合训练，在去噪前将历史观测整合为固定数量的目标视角特定token，无需显式基于深度的对应关系。结合近期时间上下文与少步蒸馏，支持从单张图像或文本提示进行流式场景探索。

Result: 在静态和动态场景的实验中，长时一致性、相机控制精度均获得显著提升，同时在分钟级探索中保持了视觉质量。

Conclusion: WorldCrafter通过相机可查询的隐式3D感知记忆机制，有效解决了视频世界模型在长时跨视角一致性上的难题，实现了高质量、可控的流式场景探索。

Abstract: Video world models enable interactive exploration of dynamic environments, yet struggle to respect prior observations over long horizons and across viewpoints. We present WorldCrafter, a video world model that learns a camera-queryable implicit 3D-aware memory for this purpose. The key insight is to let the requested viewpoint shape how multi-view evidence is compressed into the video generator's limited token budget. Trained jointly with the video generator, a memory encoder and pose-conditioned readout module integrate historical observations into a fixed set of target view-specific tokens before denoising, without explicit depth-based correspondences. By combining this memory with recent temporal context and few-step distillation, WorldCrafter enables streaming scene exploration from a single input image or text prompt. Experiments across static and dynamic scenes show substantial gains in long-horizon consistency and camera-control accuracy while preserving visual quality during minute-scale exploration.

</details>


### [220] [VideoGen-Agent: Reinforcing Video Generation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24997)
*Binxu Li,Haoyi Duan,Yuhui Zhang,Yaohui Zhang,Zihao Lin,Kaituo Feng,Suozhi Huang,Xiangyi Li,Yu Li,Chunyuan Li,Shilong Liu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将视频生成建模为多轮工具调用任务，提出多模态智能体 VideoGen-Agent，通过多任务智能体强化学习协调增强、生成与验证工具，并发布 VABench 基准，在 600 条提示上显著超越基础文生视频模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能生成高保真、时序连贯的视频，但在需要专业领域知识、特定身份保持、物理一致性或有序事件的提示上表现不佳，单一端到端生成难以满足这类复杂需求。

Method: 提出 VideoGen-Agent，一个通过多任务智能体强化学习训练的多模态智能体，能通过多轮交互调用外部工具。它在涵盖六类任务的类别均衡数据集上训练共享策略：先用教师生成的轨迹进行监督微调以建立工具使用行为，再通过强化学习精炼；采用类别感知的混合奖励评估工具调用有效性、任务适配性与生成视频质量。同时构建 VABench 基准（600 条提示，覆盖流程知识、单/多实体身份保持、物理一致性、场景构图与多镜头时序结构）。

Result: 在 VABench 上，VideoGen-Agent 相比其基座文生视频生成器提升 19.1 分（从 56.5 提升至 75.6）；进一步升级生成工具后，无需额外智能体训练即可将分数提高到 86.1。人类评分者在 84.3% 的对比中更偏好升级后的配置，而非最强的单模型基线。

Conclusion: 结果支持在各类视频生成任务中学习工具使用，并表明经过训练的智能体能够受益于后续生成工具的进步，验证了智能体式工具协调范式的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have enabled high-fidelity, temporally coherent video generation. However, these models often struggle to satisfy prompts requiring specialized knowledge, specific identities, physical consistency, or ordered events. In this paper, we present VideoGen-Agent, a multimodal agent trained through multitask agentic reinforcement learning to use external tools for video generation. The agent coordinates augmentation, generation, and verification tools through multi-turn interactions, using the prompt and intermediate observations to guide its decisions. We train a shared policy on a category-balanced dataset spanning six tasks. Supervised fine-tuning on teacher-generated trajectories establishes tool-use behavior, which is then refined through reinforcement learning. A category-aware hybrid reward evaluates tool-call validity, task-appropriate tool use, and generated video quality. We further introduce VABench, a held-out benchmark of 600 prompts covering procedural knowledge, single- and multi-entity identity preservation, physical consistency, scene composition, and multi-shot temporal structure. On VABench, VideoGen-Agent improves over its base text-to-video generator by 19.1 points, from 56.5 to 75.6. Upgrading the generation tools further raises the score to 86.1 without additional agent training. Human raters prefer the upgraded configuration over the strongest standalone baseline in 84.3% of comparisons. These results support learning tool use across video-generation tasks and show that the trained agent can benefit from subsequent advances in generation tools.

</details>


### [221] [GameHorizon Suite: Multi-Horizon Data and Evaluation in Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25001)
*Yiran Wang,Xingyilang Yin,Junfu Pu,Guangzhi Wang,Kaifeng Li,Mingyu Ouyang,Huiqiang Sun,Lingen Li,Cheng Cheng,Wangbo Yu,Honghao Chen,Xiaodong Cun,Chi-Man Pun,Zhiguo Cao,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GameHorizon，一个统一的数据与评测套件，用于在不同时间跨度上衡量视频游戏的玩法能力，包含标注流水线、大规模AAA游戏数据集与可复现基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电子游戏是衡量AI视觉理解、指令分解、目标规划与精确动作控制的理想测试平台，但现有数据集与基准存在覆盖游戏范围窄、缺少语言指令、依赖高方差在线采样等问题。

Method: GameHorizon套件包含三部分：(1) GameHorizon-Annotator，可扩展的自动化多跨度指令标注流水线；(2) GameHorizon-Data，首个大规模AAA游戏数据集，含5000小时、21款游戏、100名人类专家玩家的时间对齐视频、玩家操作与多跨度指令；(3) GameHorizon-Bench，提供可复现的离线与逐步在线测试，离线含三类主任务及诊断变体，在线验证离线分数并定位长程失败步骤。

Result: 基于该套件对47个模型进行超百万次调用评测，揭示了清晰的任务难度层级与模型能力间的显著差异。

Conclusion: GameHorizon为跨时间跨度、跨模型族群的玩法能力评估提供了标准化标尺，并将公开数据集、标注器与基准以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Modern video games provide a measurable testbed for AI models, combining abilities of visual understanding, instruction decomposition, goal planning, and precise action control over multiple temporal horizons. Existing datasets and benchmarks, however, either cover a narrow range of games, lack language instructions, or rely on high-variance online rollouts. To address these challenges, we introduce GameHorizon, a unified data and evaluation suite that measures gameplay capabilities at different horizons for diverse model families. GameHorizon Suite consists of three components. First, GameHorizon-Annotator is a scalable and automated annotation pipeline for multi-horizon instructions. Second, utilizing the pipeline, we construct GameHorizon-Data, the first large-scale AAA gameplay dataset with temporally aligned videos, player actions, and multi-horizon instructions. It comprises 5,000 hours of recordings from 21 games, collected by 100 human expert players. Third, we build GameHorizon-Bench with reproducible offline and stepwise online testing. The offline track enables reproducible evaluation using thousands of standardized questions organized into three primary tasks and a series of diagnostic variants, while the online track tests whether offline scores reflect actual gameplay capabilities and localizes failures to specific steps within long-horizon gameplay. Based on our GameHorizon Suite, we evaluate 47 models through more than one million model invocations, revealing a meaningful hierarchy of task difficulty and pronounced differences in model capabilities. Our work can provide a standardized yardstick for evaluating gameplay capabilities across horizons and model families. We will release our dataset, annotator, and benchmark to facilitate future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Recognition, Simulation, and Refusal: A Contamination-Aware Study of Classic Psychological Effects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22090)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PsyAgentBench 基准，通过 2x2x2 因子设计（范式命名/盲测 × 经典/反事实版本 × 人格操纵）在 LLM 智能体上重跑经典心理学实验，以区分"产生类人反应模式"与"真正具备该偏差"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将 LLM 输出类人反应模式直接等同于其具备相应心理偏差，这一推断逻辑存在问题。作者希望构建能分离"模式复现"与"真实偏差"的评测框架，并揭示类人效应背后不同的生成路径，而非归因于单一偏差易感性。

Method: 提出 PsyAgentBench 基准，对每个经典范式采用因子设计：提示中显式标注范式（named）或将任务包装为常规任务（blind）；使用教科书原版任务（canonical）或降低与训练数据词汇/情境重叠的结构匹配变体（counterfactual）；并与人格操纵交叉。在最多三个开放权重模型家族上完成五个范式，释放 41,904 次试验，并形式化三种范式移植失败方式。

Result: 类人效应通过性质不同的路径出现：范式标签门控与显式覆盖（Asch 从盲测 0% 到命名下 83.3%）、知识依赖的信号依赖（锚定在已知事实上为零、在虚构数量上近乎完全，也可解释为对唯一可用信号的理性使用）、标签下新内容的放大（框架效应）、稳健缺失（沉没成本）、以及安全中介选择（最小组分配中拒绝本身即为主要发现）。一句话人格改变可消除、减弱或逆转这些效应，视具体效应而定。另在两种情况下实证记录了三种移植失败：人格主导、群体崩塌、安全选择。

Conclusion: 标量化的偏差易感性评分掩盖了类人效应背后的结构化差异，作者主张以复制档案（replication profiles）替代单一评分来报告 LLM 心理偏差研究结果。

Abstract: An LLM producing the response pattern associated with a human psychological effect is not the same claim as the LLM possessing that bias. We present PsyAgentBench, a benchmark that re-runs classic psychology experiments on LLM agents under a factorial design built to separate these: each paradigm is run with the paradigm explicitly labeled in the prompt (named) or framed as a routine task (blind), and on the literal textbook version of the task (canonical) or a structurally matched variant written to reduce lexical and scenario overlap with likely training data (counterfactual), crossed with a persona manipulation. Across five completed paradigms, evaluated on up to three open-weight model families with 41,904 trials released, apparently human-like effects arise through qualitatively different routes rather than one susceptibility: paradigm-label gating with explicit override (Asch conformity, 0 percent blind to 83.3 percent named on gpt-oss-120B), knowledge-dependent signal reliance (anchoring, exactly zero on grounded facts versus near total on invented quantities, a pattern equally consistent with rational use of the only available signal), amplification on novel content under labeling (framing), robust absence (sunk cost), and safety-mediated selection where refusal itself is the primary finding (minimal-group allocation). A one-sentence persona change (agreeableness, framed as an instruction rather than a verified trait manipulation) eliminates, dampens, or reverses these effects depending on which effect it is, arguing against any single response-bias account. We further formalize, and in two cases document empirically, three ways a psychology paradigm can fail to port to LLM agents: persona dominance, population collapse, and safety selection. We argue scalar bias-susceptibility scores obscure this structure and report replication profiles instead.

</details>


### [223] [Memory That Looks Forward: A Zero-Inference Prospective Term for Personal Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22091)
*Jonathan Groff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"前瞻性记忆检索"机制：将用户的承诺/待办事项保存在显式账本中，当条目触发时对其关联的记忆项施加显著性加权，与基于嵌入的检索相乘融合，在不增加查询时推理成本的前提下大幅提升前瞻性记忆召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人记忆库检索是回溯式的：只能返回与查询相似的记忆，而对用户"已承诺要做的事"完全无视。这种盲区使得助手无法在恰当时机主动提醒用户履约，因此需要一个能在查询时零推理成本地引入前瞻信息的机制。

Method: 作者设计了"承诺账本"结构，条目带日期或触发条件；当条目触发时，与之关联的记忆项获得显著性加权（salience boost），以乘法方式混入嵌入检索得分，从而保证相关性仍占主导。评估集为参照 TriggerBench 公开结构自建的合成前瞻记忆任务集（48 段盲写对话、175 个任务）。

Result: 在困难子集上，默认混合权重下 recall@5 从 0.000 提升至 0.955，在加下限的变体下达到 1.000；在 53 个已解除承诺的任务上实现零误加权。盲写结果还给出一个适用范围发现：自然表述的承诺—触发配对中仅 17%–29% 会被嵌入相似度击败，说明该机制只在少数真实情形中起作用，且在其余情形中不造成损害。

Conclusion: 作者将预计算的承诺关联定位为分层设计的"常开底层"，其上再叠加查询时的前瞻推理层；当前结果为初步结论，评估集为作者自建，待 TriggerBench 数据公开后进行正式评测是后续承诺工作。

Abstract: Retrieval over a personal memory store is retrospective: it surfaces what resembles the query, and it is blind to what the user has committed to do. We describe a prospective term for memory retrieval that costs no inference at query time. Commitments are held in an explicit ledger as dated or trigger-conditioned entries; memory items linked to a firing entry receive a salience boost, blended multiplicatively into embedding-based retrieval so that relevance remains sovereign. On a synthetic prospective-memory task set modeled on TriggerBench's published structure (48 blind-authored dialogues, 175 tasks), the term raised recall@5 on the hard stratum from 0.000 to 0.955 at the default blend weight and to 1.000 under a floor variant, with zero false boosts across 53 resolved-commitment tasks. Blind authorship also produced a scope finding: only 17-29% of naturally phrased commitment-trigger pairs defeat embedding similarity, so the term matters on a real minority of cases and must do no harm on the rest, which it does not. We position precomputed commitment linkage as the always-on floor of a layered design whose expansion layer is query-time prospection. Results are preliminary: the evaluation set is author-constructed, and evaluation on TriggerBench proper is committed follow-up work once its data is released.

</details>


### [224] [Summarize, Judge, Refine: Decoupled Content Understanding and Policy Learning for Multimodal Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22094)
*Zeeshan Ahmed,Yang Qin,Hanqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR)双模型架构，通过自然语言接口解耦多模态理解与策略分类，实现策略无关的内容审核，支持无真实违规数据的少样本策略启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核系统将多模态理解与特定策略分类纠缠在一起，导致每次策略变更都需全流程重训练，且因多媒体难以有效增强而面临标签稀缺问题。

Method: SJR采用两个模型：多模态Content Model生成结构化文本摘要，纯文本Policy Model依据策略定义对摘要分类。通过迭代协同训练循环，用GRPO精炼Content Model以产生策略相关摘要；并利用文本空间增强生成对抗性摘要变体（此增强路径在原始多媒体上不可行），实现少样本策略启动。每个决策均基于人类可读摘要，可解释性作为结构性副产品。

Result: 在误导性广告检测任务上，SJR相比零样本思维链基线取得+23.6%相对非误导性F1提升，优于端到端SFT、STaR/RFT和RLFT。值得注意的是，一个仅用合成生成正类数据、训练中未使用任何真实违规样本的变体，在违规F1上与全数据模型相对差距仅0.2%。

Conclusion: 新策略无需任何真实违规数据即可上线部署，验证了SJR在解耦架构与数据高效性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Content moderation systems traditionally entangle multimodal understanding with policy-specific classification, requiring full pipeline retraining for every policy change and suffering from label scarcity since multimedia cannot be meaningfully augmented. We propose Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR), a two-model architecture that decouples these concerns via a natural language interface: a multimodal Content Model produces structured text summaries, and a text-only Policy Model classifies them against policy definitions. An iterative co-training loop refines the Content Model via GRPO to produce policy-relevant summaries, while text-space augmentation generates adversarial summary variants---an augmentation pathway impossible on raw multimedia---enabling few-shot policy bootstrap. Every decision is grounded in a human-readable summary, providing interpretability as a structural byproduct. On misleading advertisement detection, SJR achieves +23.6\% relative non-misleading F1 over a zero-shot chain-of-thought baseline, outperforming end-to-end SFT, STaR/RFT, and RLFT. Notably, a variant trained on zero real violating examples---with all positive-class data synthetically generated---matches the full-data model within 0.2\% relative on violating F1, demonstrating that new policies can launch without any real violation data.

</details>


### [225] [AI-inferred expressed well-being and collective-action discourse in climate-change campaigns on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22096)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 气候活动常以关注度和动员程度评估，但其伴随的福祉语汇研究不足。本文分析19个出现年份、四类气候活动（地球日、地球一小时、全球气候行动日、世界环境日）共364,118条Twitter/X帖子，采用版本化加权词汇模型测量幸福、未来导向希望、集体能力、痛苦与行动语汇。发现活动期幸福语汇比活动前基线高9.02个百分点，但行动语汇下降10.75个百分点，呈"幸福—行动背离"；幸福估计在构成与去重检验中稳健，但19簇wild bootstrap下精度较低；较幸福的源帖获得匹配转推级联的几率更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有气候活动评估多聚焦注意力与动员，较少关注活动所伴随的福祉类语汇。活动期是否改变积极情感与希望、幸福感是否与行动语汇一致，尚属未解问题。

Method: 选取地球日、地球一小时、全球气候行动日、世界环境日共19个出现年份的364,118条公开Twitter/X帖子，设置活动前30天、活动期、活动后三类窗口；使用版本化加权词汇模型估计幸福、未来导向希望、集体能力、痛苦与行动语汇；并辅以构成检验、文本去重检验、19簇wild bootstrap，以及源帖幸福度与匹配转推级联的关联分析。

Result: 活动期幸福语汇流行度比活动前基线高9.02个百分点，而同场次配对对比显示行动语汇下降10.75个百分点，呈现幸福—行动背离。幸福估计在构成与文本去重检验中保持正向，但在19簇wild bootstrap下精度下降。较幸福的源帖被观察到匹配转推级联的几率更低。

Conclusion: 气候活动期的公共话语在情感上更积极，却未必伴随更多行动语汇；幸福表达与行动动员之间存在背离，且幸福度更高的内容传播级联更弱。研究提示以情感与传播指标评价气候活动时需谨慎，福祉语汇应被纳入活动评估视野。

Abstract: Climate campaigns are often evaluated through attention and mobilization, but less is known about the well-being language that accompanies them. Whether campaign periods alter positive affect and hope, and whether happiness aligns with action language, remains unresolved. We analysed 364,118 public Twitter/X posts from Earth Day, Earth Hour, Global Climate Action Day and World Environment Day in 19 occurrence-years, using 30-day pre-event, event and post-event windows. A versioned weighted lexical model estimated happiness, future-oriented hope, collective capability, distress and action language. Event-period happiness prevalence was 9.02 percentage points higher than the pre-event baseline , whereas paired occurrence contrasts showed a 10.75-point decline in action language, indicating a happiness--action divergence. The happiness estimate remained positive across composition and text-deduplication checks, but was less precise under a 19-cluster wild bootstrap. Happier source posts had lower odds of an observed matched retweet cascade.

</details>


### [226] [Token Signatures of Code: Comparing Coding Behaviors Across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22097)
*Junpeng Wang,Yuzhong Chen,Menghai Pan,Uday Singh Saini,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIC，一种基于token频率分析的视觉分析方案，通过可解释决策树区分两个LLM的代码样本，并定义鲁棒性与集中度两个新指标，配合交互式可视分析系统探索LLM编码行为的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码能力评估主要依赖pass@k等性能指标，随着模型普遍达到基线水平，这类指标区分度下降；而"不同LLM在编码行为上如何差异"这一关键问题仍未被充分探索。

Method: CLIC将每个代码样本表示为token频率特征向量，训练可解释决策树区分两个LLM的代码集合；在分类准确率之外定义鲁棒性（逐步移除最具判别性token后两模型是否仍可区分）和集中度（差异由少数主导token驱动还是分散于众多token）两个指标；并开发交互式可视分析系统，支持在多尺度（模型对、任务、分词层级）比较中导航、定位兴趣对并下钻到判别性token及其代码上下文。

Result: 在22个Kaggle机器学习任务上对10个LLM进行案例研究，揭示了可用于LLM选择与提示工程的可操作洞见。

Conclusion: CLIC通过token频率与可解释决策树，将编码评估从单一性能指标拓展到行为层面的可区分性分析，为LLM选择与提示工程提供新视角。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on coding tasks has primarily focused on performance metrics such as pass@k. As LLMs continue to advance, many models now meet baseline performance requirements, reducing the discriminative power of performance-based evaluation alone. Yet a key question remains largely unexplored: how do LLMs differ in their coding behavior? We propose CLIC (Code Learning for Identification and Comparison), a visual analytics approach that characterizes LLM coding behavior through token-frequency analysis. CLIC represents each code sample as a feature vector of token frequencies and trains an interpretable decision tree to separate two LLMs' code sets. Beyond classification accuracy, we define two new metrics: robustness, which measures whether the two LLMs remain distinguishable as their most-discriminative tokens are progressively removed, and concentration, which measures whether the difference is driven by a few dominant tokens or spread across many. Interpreting numerous pairwise comparisons (across LLM pairs, tasks, and tokenization levels) and tracing the full analytical chain form an inherently multi-scale, hypothesis-driven exploration task. We therefore develop an interactive visual analytics system to navigate the comparison landscape, identify pairs of interest, and drill down into discriminative tokens and their code contexts. Case studies comparing 10 LLMs across 22 Kaggle ML tasks reveal actionable insights for LLM selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [227] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TreeSpark：利用草稿模型已有的 Markov head 读出父条件分布并校准为边接受率，以路径存活率为统一准则驱动最优优先扩展、每轮停止与负载自适应服务策略，实现无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块草稿器（block drafter）一次前向即可产出整块草稿，成本极低；草稿树（draft tree）理论上可进一步在一次目标模型前向中验证多条备选续写，但现有构建方式按逐位置边缘分布排序候选，忽略了候选所延展的父节点，导致在半自回归草稿器上扩大树宽只会增加排序错误的节点；同时固定尺寸的树也无法适应每轮解码可支持的推测量以及不同服务负载。

Method: TreeSpark 以极低开销从草稿器现有的 Markov head 读出父条件分布，将其校准为边接受率估计；随后以路径存活率（path survival）作为统一准则，指导三件事：最优优先的树扩展、每轮停止判断、以及负载自适应的服务策略。在采样上采用无放回地采样兄弟节点，并在递归拒绝采样中使用相匹配的残差分布，从而在任意温度下保持解码无损。

Result: 自适应树在任意温度下均优于同等预算的固定树；在同一草稿器上对比调优后的链式（chain）基线，TreeSpark 每轮多接受 15–25% 的草稿 token，单请求墙钟时间解码快 8–14%；在负载上升时可将树优雅地退化回链式结构。

Conclusion: TreeSpark 通过父条件分布与路径存活率驱动的自适应树构建，解决了草稿树排序失准与固定预算僵化的问题，在保持任意温度下无损的前提下取得稳定的推理加速，并具备随负载弹性调节的能力。代码与工件已开源。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [228] [A framework for recipe data structure with applications for culinary and nutritional insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22099)
*Mansi Goel,Sumit Bhagat,Saloni Srivastava,Malav Patel,Shlok Vinodkumar Mehroliya,Ganesh Bagler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对食谱数据缺乏统一可查询表示的问题，提出RecipeDB2框架，将食谱结构化为类型化食材实体，关联营养数据库并标注地域文化与饮食背景，构建了涵盖128,942个食谱、35,474种食材的结构化数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烹饪是从原始食材到菜肴的复杂转化过程，但食谱多以自由文本形式存在，人类可读却无法直接计算。现有食谱数据集只捕获了碎片化信息，缺少一个统一的可查询模式，将食谱的结构化食材组成、地域文化来源与营养特征联系起来。论文旨在填补这一表示鸿沟。

Method: 论文提出一个食谱数据结构框架，将每个食谱分解为类型化食材实体，将其映射到参考营养数据库，并标注地域文化与饮食背景。具体实现RecipeDB2：用基于Transformer的命名实体模型将食材短语解析为七种烹饪属性；通过BERT嵌入策略将食材链接到USDA参考表（在人工审核的200个最高频食材集上F1=87.90），每个映射食材产出148个营养参数；用随机森林分类器将34个食材类别传播到整个词汇表；并用确定性、保守的规则集为每个食谱分配饮食风格。

Result: 构建了RecipeDB2，包含来自32个地区、99个国家的128,942个食谱和35,474种食材。食材与USDA参考表链接在最高频200个食材的人工审核集上达到F1=87.90，每个映射食材获得148个营养参数。

Conclusion: RecipeDB2展示了一个可扩展的框架，使食谱可计算，将长期被视为艺术而非定量对象的烹饪遗产转化为数据驱动的分析对象。

Abstract: Cooking is a complex process that transforms raw ingredients into delicious and nutritious dishes, yet the recipes that encode this process remain largely free text; readable by people but not directly computable. Existing recipe collections capture fragments of this information, but no shared representation links a recipe's structured ingredient composition, its geo-cultural provenance, and its nutritional profile within a single queryable schema. We address this representation gap by formalizing a framework for recipe data structure that decomposes each recipe into typed ingredient entities, grounds those entities in a reference nutritional database, and annotates them with geo-cultural and dietary context. We present RecipeDB2, a structured compilation of 128,942 recipes with 35,474 ingredients from 32 regions and 99 countries. Ingredient phrases are parsed into seven culinary attributes using a transformer-based named-entity model; ingredients are linked to the USDA reference tables through a BERT embedding strategy (F1 = 87.90 on a manually adjudicated set of the 200 most frequent ingredients), yielding 148 nutritional parameters per mapped ingredient; a Random Forest classifier propagates 34 ingredient categories across the full vocabulary; and a deterministic, conservative rule set assigns each recipe a dietary style. Through RecipeDB2 (https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/), we demonstrate a scalable framework for making recipes computable, turning culinary heritage (long treated as an artistic rather than a quantitative object) into a data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [229] [AdaMem: Adaptive Memory Token Allocation for Soft Compression in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22100)
*Artem Sakhno,Grigorii Davydenko,Omar Zoloev,Julia Belikova,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaMem是一种相关性引导的软压缩框架，根据段落相关性估计动态分配固定记忆令牌预算，在多个问答基准上优于均匀分配方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中处理大量长段落成本高且引入干扰信息。软压缩通过将段落编码为紧凑的连续记忆嵌入来应对，但现有方法对每个保留段落分配相同数量的记忆嵌入，忽略了段落的查询相关性差异。

Method: 提出AdaMem，将学习到的段落相关性估计映射为查询相关的固定记忆令牌预算分配。共享的查询条件压缩器一次生成连续段落记忆和相关性分数；确定性分配规则给高分段落更多记忆令牌，并可省略低分段落。

Result: 在六个开放域QA基准上一致优于OSCAR（均匀分配的软压缩基线）及其他方法。16×压缩下子串匹配最多提升3.2点（5.5%），平均相对增益3.4%；64×压缩下平均相对增益达14.6%，PopQA上最高提升9.8点（19.7%）。在最多比全上下文基线低4×推理延迟下达到未压缩的答案质量。

Conclusion: 相关性引导的记忆分配在检索池大且记忆预算紧张时特别有效；AdaMem保持与均匀压缩基线相当的高效性，同时实现比全上下文推理最多低4×的延迟。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models with retrieved evidence, but processing many long passages is costly and can introduce distracting information. Soft compression addresses this challenge by encoding passages as compact sequences of continuous memory embeddings before generation. However, existing methods typically assign each retained passage an identical number of memory embeddings, irrespective of its query-specific relevance. To address this, we propose AdaMem, a relevance-guided soft-compression framework that maps learned passage-relevance estimates to a query-dependent allocation of a fixed memory-token budget. A shared query-conditioned compressor produces both continuous passage memories and relevance scores in a single pass; a deterministic allocation rule assigns more memory tokens to higher-scoring passages and can omit low-scoring ones. Across six open-domain QA benchmarks, AdaMem consistently outperforms OSCAR (the closely matched soft-compression baseline that uses uniform allocation) as well as other soft-compression methods at matched memory budgets. Under standard 16$\times$ compression, AdaMem improves sub-string match by up to 3.2 points (5.5%) over uniform allocation baseline, with an average relative gain of 3.4%; under aggressive 64$\times$ compression the average relative gain grows to 14.6%, with a maximum of 9.8 points (19.7%) on PopQA. AdaMem matches the answer quality of the uncompressed at up to 4$\times$ lower inference latency than full context baseline. AdaMem retains an efficiency profile comparable to the uniform-compression baseline, while achieving up to $4\times$ lower inference latency than full-context inference. Thus, relevance-guided memory allocation is particularly effective when retrieval pools are large and the available memory budget is tight.

</details>


### [230] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Bhaskar Sen,Nasim Sabetpour,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文投毒"现象：随干扰上下文增多，大模型定位决定性证据的能力下降；将其形式化为注意力中的极值干扰问题，并给出有限样本上界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能处理越来越长的提示，但当无关或易混淆的上下文加入时，其定位与使用决定性证据的能力可能退化。作者希望解释并刻画这一长上下文退化现象的机制。

Method: 将"上下文投毒"形式化为注意力中的极值干扰：决定性证据得分有上界，而有效干扰项的最大得分随其数量增长。在softmax检索抽象下推导有限样本上界，分析其与得分混叠、位置混叠和softmax稀释的联系，并开展受控实验。

Result: 理论表明，在基准率之上维持固定准确率目标，需要证据间隔按Ω(√log N)增长（N为有效干扰项数量，而非原始上下文长度）。实验显示：在嵌入难负例时，检索准确率随总上下文增大而下降；固定上下文长度下，同格式条件在测试的干扰构造中产生最大准确率降幅；检索门控可改善证据使用，但其净收益取决于能否保持证据召回率。

Conclusion: 结果指向若干改进方向：证据瓶颈、抗混叠表示、先检索后推理架构、验证器中介记忆以及对比式抗投毒训练。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [231] [DeepInstructor: An Agentic AI Instructor for Experience-Driven Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22104)
*Rongcan Pei,Fang Guo,Qinglin Qi,Qi Zhu,Yun Luo,Jianhao Yan,Minjun Zhu,Qiujie Xie,Dehong Zheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DeepInstructor，一个将科研想法评估建模为"对结构化学术经验进行推理"的智能体框架，并配套发布 DeepInstruct 数据集，显著提升了与人类判断的对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型能大规模生成科研想法，瓶颈已从"想法生成"转移到"想法评估"。现有评估器主要依赖参数化知识或非结构化检索，其判断缺乏人类导师那种基于经验的推理依据。因此作者希望让想法评估落地于显式的、结构化的学术经验推理之上。

Method: 作者构建了一个"经验图"（Experience Graph），来源于 58,607 条同行评审；并采用基于 ReAct 的智能体，围绕新颖性、重要性、可行性等维度检索针对性证据，从而实现可追溯的评估。同时引入 DeepInstruct 数据集，包含在上述维度上受控的成对比较样本。

Result: 实验显示 DeepInstructor 显著优于现有基线，在与人类判断对齐的 Hit@1 和 Hit@2 指标上分别提升 24.4% 和 29.7%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，科学想法评估可以建立在"对结构化学术经验的显式推理"之上，为自动化科研发现中的评估环节提供了可追溯、经验驱动的解决路径。

Abstract: As automated scientific discovery advances, Large Language Models (LLMs) can now generate research ideas at an unprecedented scale, shifting the bottleneck from idea generation to idea evaluation. Existing evaluators mainly rely on parametric LLM knowledge or unstructured retrieval, producing judgments that lack the experience-grounded reasoning used by human instructors. To address this, we propose DeepInstructor, an agentic framework that formulates idea evaluation as reasoning over structured scholarly experience. DeepInstructor constructs an Experience Graph from 58,607 peer reviews and employs a ReAct-based agent to retrieve dimension-specific evidence for traceable evaluation. We further introduce DeepInstruct, a dataset with controlled pairwise comparisons across novelty, significance, and feasibility. Experiments show that DeepInstructor substantially outperforms existing baselines, improving Hit@1 and Hit@2 alignment with human judgments by 24.4% and 29.7%, respectively. Our findings suggest that scientific idea evaluation can be grounded in explicit reasoning over structured scholarly experience

</details>


### [232] [Evaluating Fine-Tuned and Base Language Models in Maternal and Vaccination Healthcare for African Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22110)
*Abdulquddus Ajibade,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Iyinoluwa Animasaun,Chioma Nwakanma-Akanno,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Oluwafunke Akinbuwa,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种领域的LLM进行领域微调评估：MamaBot-Llama显著优于基座模型，而Vax-Llama性能反而下降，说明微调效果高度依赖数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可改善低资源地区的医疗信息传递，但可能给出不准确或不符合文化背景的建议，因此需要评估领域微调在母婴健康与疫苗接种场景中的实际效果与安全性。

Method: 采用200个医疗问题（母婴健康与疫苗接种各100个，每领域覆盖5个子领域）；MamaBot-Llama与Vax-Llama分别基于36,000余条母婴健康和9,000余条疫苗接种问答对，使用LoRA进行微调；由两名尼日利亚执业医师用5点Likert量表独立评分；采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行配对比较。

Result: 效果因领域而异。MamaBot-Llama在所有指标上显著优于基座模型，总体提升4.9%（p<.001），临床可信度提升7%、医学准确性提升5%，严重问题减少50%，临床医生在78%的案例中更偏好该模型。相反，Vax-Llama总体下降5.2%（p<.001），严重问题增加192%，安全担忧增加400%。

Conclusion: 当基于高质量的临床医生审核数据时，领域微调可提升医疗LLM表现，但数据质量不足时也可能导致性能退化。临床部署前必须进行严格的领域特定验证。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) can improve healthcare information delivery in low-resource settings but may produce inaccurate or culturally inappropriate advice. This study evaluated domain-specific fine-tuning for maternal health and vaccination in Nigeria. Objective: To compare HelpMum's MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama with Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct for accuracy, safety, clarity, contextual appropriateness, and trustworthiness. Methods: We evaluated 200 healthcare questions, 100 each for maternal health and vaccination, across five subdomains per domain. MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation on over 36,000 maternal health and 9,000 vaccination question-answer pairs, respectively. Two Nigerian licensed physicians independently rated responses using a 5-point Likert scale. Paired comparisons used Wilcoxon signed-rank tests. Results: Performance varied by domain. MamaBot-Llama significantly outperformed the base model across all criteria, with a 4.9% overall improvement (p < .001), including gains in clinical trustworthiness (+7%) and medical accuracy (+5%). Critical issues decreased by 50%, and clinicians preferred it in 78% of cases. In contrast, Vax-Llama showed a 5.2% overall decline (p < .001), with critical issues increasing by 192% and safety concerns by 400%. Conclusions: Domain-specific fine-tuning can improve healthcare LLM performance when based on high-quality, clinician-curated data, but may also degrade performance when dataset quality is inadequate. Rigorous domain-specific validation is essential before clinical deployment. Physician evaluators provided informed consent, and chatbot logs were anonymized. Keywords: Large language models; Fine-tuning; Maternal health; Vaccination; Healthcare AI; Low-resource settings; Nigeria; Model evaluation; LoRA; Medical accuracy

</details>


### [233] [Beyond the Text: Verifying That Agent-Written Papers Are Backed by Their Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22111)
*Qiuhong Shen,Benlong Wu,Hanjin Liu,Yuang Qi,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReAgent，一个自动化审计框架，用于评估 LLM 智能体生成的研究文档与其关联代码仓库之间的一致性，结合静态审计与动态审计来发现硬编码指标、未实现方法、无支撑实验结果等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体已能自主开展研究并产出文档、代码与实验，但其报告结论是否被实现和执行证据一致支撑尚缺乏检验。现有评审实践侧重文本质量，无法可靠识别硬编码指标、方法未实现、结果无支撑等不一致问题。

Method: ReAgent 从研究文档中构建科学主张的结构化表示，以此引导仓库分析与证据收集：静态审计检查所声称的方法、实现与实验配置是否一致反映在仓库中；动态审计执行相关实验并收集执行证据以评估实证结论。两者结合可发现单一视角难以察觉的不一致（如复现了报告数值但偏离所述方法）。收集的证据与审计决策被组织成结构化、可追溯的仓库级审计报告。

Result: 在人工构建的智能体生成研究文档—仓库配对基准上评估，并与代表性静态基线和基于复现的基线比较，实验结果表明 ReAgent 能有效识别报告结论与支撑仓库证据之间的不一致。

Conclusion: ReAgent 通过静态与动态证据结合的自动化审计，可有效检验智能体生成研究的文档—仓库一致性，并借助结构化审计报告实现证据透明可追溯，为科研产出的可信度评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly capable of conducting research autonomously, producing research documents alongside the code and experiments that ostensibly support them. Yet whether the reported findings are consistently supported by corresponding implementations and execution evidence remains largely unexplored: existing review practices primarily assess textual quality and cannot reliably identify inconsistencies such as hard-coded metrics, unimplemented methods, or unsupported experimental results. We present ReAgent, an automated auditing framework for assessing the consistency between agent-generated research documents and their associated repositories. ReAgent constructs structured representations of scientific claims from research documents and uses them to guide repository analysis and evidence collection. Static auditing examines whether claimed methodologies, implementations, and experimental configurations are consistently reflected in the repository, while dynamic auditing executes relevant experiments and collects execution evidence to assess empirical findings. By combining static analysis with dynamic evidence, ReAgent identifies inconsistencies that may remain hidden under either perspective alone, such as experiments that reproduce reported numbers while deviating from the claimed methodology. The collected evidence and audit decisions are organized into a structured repository-level audit report, enabling transparent evidence traceability. We evaluate ReAgent on a manually curated benchmark of agent-generated research document--repository pairs and compare it against representative static and reproduction-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that ReAgent effectively identifies inconsistencies between reported research findings and their supporting repository evidence.

</details>


### [234] [Privacy Personalization Trade offs in LLMs: The Impact of Stylometric Signal Reduction on User-Specific Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22112)
*Muhammed Nazmul Arefin,Omar Jamal Hammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM个性化生成中的"文体计量再识别"风险：通过匿名化用户画像，发现个性化偏好大幅下降（平均仅13.0%），而语义保留仍高达94.8%，揭示了隐私与个性化之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成具有高文体保真度的个性化文本，但支撑个性化的个人数据常含有人口统计、文化及文体标记，引发文体计量再识别的隐私担忧。作者希望探究：减少可识别的文体信号是否会损害LLM文本生成的个性化效果。

Method: 提出一个受控框架，基于LaMP-7 Twitter基准隔离LLM个性化中的文体计量信号。实验抽取250名用户，对比两种设定：以原始画像为条件的改写，与以匿名化转换画像（系统性地中和人口统计标识、文化引用、个人细节及非正式语言线索）为条件的改写。输出由两个独立LLM评判员及一项互补的人工评估进行评价，并采用成对比较。

Result: 成对评估显示，以原始画像为条件的输出与人类作者的真实文本几乎无法区分，表明现代LLM能以高保真度复现作者写作风格。相比之下，匿名化画像下的模型输出偏好度平均降至13.0%，而语义上下文保留仍高达94.8%。人工评估研究证实了相同的模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了清晰的隐私—个性化权衡，凸显出对隐私感知型个性化方法的需求——此类方法应能在保留语义的同时抑制可识别的文体信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated the ability to generate user-specific text with high stylistic fidelity. However, the personal data that enables such personalization frequently embeds demographic, cultural, and stylistic markers that raises concerns about stylometric re- identification. This paper investigates whether reducing identifiable stylistic signals affects personalization in text generation by LLMs. We introduce a controlled framework to isolate stylometric signals in LLM personalization using the LaMP-7 Twitter benchmark. Experiments on 250 sampled users compare two settings: paraphrasing conditioned on the original profile and paraphrasing conditioned on an anonymized converted profile in which demographic identifiers, cultural references, personal details, and informal linguistic cues have been systematically neutralized. Outputs are assessed by two independent LLM judges and a complementary human evaluation. Our pairwise evaluation shows that outputs conditioned on original profiles are nearly indistinguishable from human-authored ground truth, indicating that modern LLMs can closely reproduce an author's writing style with sufficient fidelity. In contrast, preference for model outputs with anonymized profiles drops to 13.0% on average, while semantic context preservation remains high at 94.8%. A study with human evaluators confirms the same pattern. These findings reveal a clear privacy-personalization trade-off and highlight the need for privacy-aware personalization methods that retain meaning while suppressing identifying stylistic signals.

</details>


### [235] [An Empirical Cost Attribution of Context-Compression Gateways in Multi-Turn Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22114)
*Luzhuo Chen,Jiayu Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在真实多轮编程智能体中插桩压缩网关，论文将token账单分解为工具schema过滤、内容压缩与历史摘要三个杠杆，并指出单轮压缩基准与多轮成本节约并不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基准常报告激进压缩可保留任务质量，因此被默认能节省真实多轮智能体的成本；但"压缩能保质量"与"基准表现好"并不意味着"压缩文件读取能省钱"。论文旨在检验这一被普遍假定的结论。

Method: 在生产压缩网关Paritok中插桩，连接编程智能体(Claude Code、Codex)与前沿LLM(Claude Sonnet、GPT-5)，在受控A/B运行下将真实会话的token账单分解为三个独立杠杆：工具schema过滤、文件读取与工具输出的内容压缩、历史摘要；并考察非破坏性网关的按需召回机制。

Result: 工具schema过滤每轮移除固定块(约21K–57K tokens)，随轮数N线性增长，是唯一稳定为正的杠杆；内容压缩每轮仅省约2%的缓存定价前缀，但因压缩读取累积并反复重发，累计节约约3350*N^2 tokens呈二次增长，约6轮后超过工具过滤，直至被上下文窗口封顶。非破坏性召回每段成本固定有界，重度召回会逐步花掉累积节约。单轮基准(86.5% SWE-bench质量保留、25.7%压缩率)与多轮成本正交，不能作为省钱依据。

Conclusion: 单轮压缩质量基准不能外推为多轮智能体的成本节约；真正的省钱关键在于随轮数线性增长的工具schema过滤，以及随轮数二次增长但受上下文窗口限制的内容压缩，召回成本则固定有界。论文据此提炼出token节约投入方向的可操作配方。

Abstract: Context compression is widely proposed as a way to cut the token bill of LLM coding agents, and public benchmarks report that aggressive compression preserves task-solving quality. These two facts do not imply the third one commonly assumed: that compressing file reads saves money in a real multi-turn agent. We instrument a production compression gateway (Paritok) between coding agents (Claude Code, Codex) and frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet, GPT-5), and decompose the token bill of real sessions into three independent levers: tool-schema filtering, content compression of file reads and tool output, and history summarization. Measured in isolation under controlled A/B runs, the three save at fundamentally different rates. Tool-schema filtering removes a fixed block every turn, roughly 21K-57K tokens on a typical turn; it is linear in the turn count N and the only unambiguously and reproducibly positive lever. Content compression saves only about 2% of the cache-priced prefix per turn, but compressed reads accumulate in history and are re-sent on every later turn, so its cumulative saving grows quadratically, about 3350*N^2 tokens (measured), overtaking the fixed tool-filter saving within roughly 6 turns until the context window caps it. A non-destructive gateway lets the agent pull original bytes back on demand; each recall re-sends exactly the one segment just compressed away, so its cost is fixed and bounded rather than a multiplicative blowup, and heavy recall spends the accumulated saving back one segment at a time. Finally, a strong single-shot compression benchmark - 86.5% of SWE-bench quality retained at a 25.7% compression rate, achieved by the model this gateway deploys (Paritok-4B, reported separately) - is orthogonal to multi-turn agent cost and must not be cited as a cost-saving argument. We distill the results into an actionable recipe for where token-saving effort pays off.

</details>


### [236] [Evaluation Awareness Shifts from Format to Context with Model Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22119)
*Navraj Singh,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究模型如何检测评估情境（评估意识），发现小模型依赖提示格式敏感性，大模型依赖高阶推理，并提出双重路径干预方法以抑制评估意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估意识对模型评估构成前所未有的威胁，但模型检测评估的机制仍不明确，尤其不清楚小模型与大模型之间的机制差异。

Method: 评估了Gemma 3（1B、4B、12B）、Phi-3（Mini、Medium）和Llama-3 8B，采用思维链分析、表征探测和积分梯度归因；据此提出结合提示净化和激活反操纵的双重路径干预方法。

Result: 在200个高评估意识提示上，方法平均行为翻转率达到70.58%，持续优于单独使用任一干预手段。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了紧凑语言模型中评估意识的发展机制，表明有效缓解需同时处理提示层面和表征层面的信号。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness poses an unprecedented threat to model evaluation, but the mechanisms by which models detect it remain unknown. This study focuses on determining this and identifying contrasting mechanisms between smaller and larger models. While smaller models use the prompt's format sensitivity to detect evaluation, larger models often rely on higher-order reasoning to detect it. We evaluated Gemma 3 (1B, 4B, and 12B), Phi-3 (Mini and Medium), and Llama-3 8B using Chain-of-Thought analysis, representation probing, and Integrated Gradients attribution. Motivated by these findings, we propose a dual-pathway intervention that combines prompt sanitization with activation counter-steering to suppress both external evaluation triggers and their internal representations. Across 200 highly evaluation-aware prompts, our method achieves an average behavioral flip rate of 70.58\%, consistently outperforming either intervention alone. These results provide new insights into how evaluation awareness develops in compact language models and suggest that effective mitigation requires jointly addressing both prompt-level and representation-level signals.Datasets and codebase can be found in this \href{https://github.com/chahal-navi/Evaluation-Awareness-Compact-LLMs/tree/main}{Github Repository.}

</details>


### [237] [Balancing Reasoning and Hardware Constraints in RAG Pipelines for Ukrainian Multi-Domain Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22124)
*Illya Havrylov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了参加 UNLP 2026 多领域文档理解共享任务的系统，在9小时离线限制下，采用资源高效的混合RAG管线，最终获得私有测试集0.8095分，排名第10（共15支队伍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务要求在严格的9小时离线Kaggle执行限时内，从多样化的乌克兰语PDF文档中提取精确答案、文档ID和页码。评估中发现扫描文档的OCR成为严重瓶颈，单线程顺序执行耗时5-7小时，导致留给LLM推理的时间仅约2小时需处理500个问题，因此必须构建资源高效且稳定的管线以避免超时。

Method: 开发了资源高效的混合检索增强生成（RAG）管线，使用BM25、BGE-M3和Cross-Encoder重排序。放弃参数庞大的推理模型（如DeepSeek R1，因持续超时），改用经4-bit量化的LapaLLM 12B模型，通过llama.cpp在双NVIDIA T4 GPU上运行，优先保证管线稳定性而非多步推理。

Result: 系统在私有测试集上获得0.8095分，在15支活跃队伍中排名第10。

Conclusion: 在严格时间约束下，资源受限系统中管线稳定性比多步推理更重要；通过量化小型模型与高效混合检索策略，可在有限计算预算内完成多领域文档理解任务。

Abstract: This paper describes the system submitted to the UNLP 2026 Shared Task on Multi-Domain Document Understanding. The challenge required extracting precise answers, document IDs, and page numbers from a diverse corpus of Ukrainian PDF documents within a strict 9-hour offline Kaggle execution limit. During evaluation on the hidden private test set, optical character recognition (OCR) of scanned documents emerged as a severe bottleneck, consuming 5-7 hours of the total time budget due to sequential single-threaded execution. This overhead strictly limited the remaining time for Large Language Model (LLM) inference to approximately two hours for 500 questions. To guarantee pipeline completion without timeouts, we developed a resource-efficient Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipeline utilizing BM25, BGE-M3, and Cross-Encoder reranking. Rather than deploying parameter-heavy reasoning models (e.g., DeepSeek R1) which consistently timed out, we utilized a 4-bit quantized LapaLLM 12B model via llama.cpp on dual NVIDIA T4 GPUs. Prioritizing pipeline stability over multi-step reasoning, our system achieved a Private Score of 0.8095, placing 10th out of 15 active teams.

</details>


### [238] [Type-Driven Tokenization for Brahmic Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22125)
*Sai Hemanth Kapila,Rakshika Bagavathy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型的标准分词器在处理婆罗米系文字时会产生畸变文本，因为婆罗米正字法是"部分半群"而非英语那样的"半群"。作者用 Agda 形式化这一区别，将合法 token 建模为迁移系统中的链，推导出可证明正确的 fixToken 函数，并落地为 SentencePiece 补丁和 Rust 预分词库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分词器（如 SentencePiece）应用于婆罗米系元音附标文字（Devanagari、Telugu、Tamil、Kannada 等）时会产生畸形文本。其根本原因在于这些分词器违反了仅在非字母文字中出现的正字法约束：英语正字法构成半群（任意两个合法 token 可自由拼接），而婆罗米正字法只是部分半群（并非所有拼接都产生合法字符串），因此需要形式化刻画并据此修复。

Method: 1) 在 Agda 中对半群与部分半群的区分进行形式化；2) 将合法的婆罗米 token 建模为迁移系统中的链；3) 由此推导出一个可证明正确的 fixToken 函数，将任意候选 token 扩展至符合正字法边界；4) 将该形式化推导转化为 SentencePiece 的实际补丁，以及一个独立的基于 Rust 的预分词器库。

Result: 所提出的 fixToken 函数在形式化层面可证明正确，并且其实际实现（SentencePiece 补丁与 Rust 预分词库）消除了跨 Indic 文字中观察到的分词错误。

Conclusion: 通过把婆罗米正字法刻画为部分半群并用形式化方法推导修复函数，本文在理论与实践之间建立了桥梁，成功修复了主流分词器在 Indic 文字上的畸形输出问题。

Abstract: Standard tokenizers used in large language models produce malformed text when applied to Brahmic scripts. They are a family of abugidas, writing systems whose consonants carry an inherent vowel that dependent marks can modify. They include Devanagari, Telugu, Tamil, Kannada, and others. The underlying issue is that these tokenizers violate orthographic constraints that do not arise in alphabetic scripts like English. We observe that while English orthography forms a \emph{semigroup} (any two valid tokens can be freely concatenated), Brahmic orthography forms a \emph{partial semigroup}: not every concatenation yields a valid string. We formalise this distinction in Agda, model valid Brahmic tokens as chains in a transition system, and derive a provably correct \texttt{fixToken} function that extends any candidate token to respect orthographic boundaries. We then show how this formal derivation translates into a practical patch for SentencePiece as well as a standalone Rust-based pre-tokenizer library, eliminating the observed errors across Indic scripts.

</details>


### [239] [Beyond Accuracy and Surface Fluency: Risk-Sensitive Evaluation of LLMs for Legal Clause Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22127)
*Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出评估LLM生成合同条款的实证研究设计与框架，采用CLAUSE分类与LENS-CRAFT九维评分，并以"最大严重性原则"暴露单一决定性法律缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益用于起草合同语言，但传统的准确率或偏好类评估与法律起草需求错配：条款可能流畅且文风精致，却遗漏必要例外条款、以不可执行方式分配风险、假设不适用的管辖法域，或使当事人面临监管责任。

Method: 评估四个模型（Claude Haiku 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、GPT 5.4 Nano、Qwen 3.5 Flash），覆盖22类合同条款与34种具法律动机的失效模式。结合两套框架：CLAUSE按法律功能与失效目标对提示分类；LENS-CRAFT从九个法律质量维度对输出打分。不采用维度分数平均，而是应用"最大严重性原则"以保留单一决定性法律缺陷的可见性。

Result: 论文提供评估协议、分类体系、分析计划，以及用于报告实证结果的结果结构，未报告具体实验数值。

Conclusion: 法律AI评估应从总体准确率转向条款特定、失效模式驱动且风险敏感的评估方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to draft contractual language, yet conventional accuracy or preference-based evaluations are poorly matched to legal drafting. A clause may be fluent and stylistically polished while still omitting an essential carve-out, allocating risk in an unenforceable way, assuming an inapplicable jurisdiction, or exposing a party to regulatory liability. This paper presents a empirical study design and framework for evaluating LLM-generated contract clauses. The study evaluates four models - Claude Haiku 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, GPT 5.4 Nano, and Qwen 3.5 Flash, across 22 contract clause categories and 34 legally-motivated failure modes. We combine two evaluation frameworks: CLAUSE, which classifies prompts by legal function and failure target, and LENS-CRAFT, which scores outputs across nine legal-quality dimensions. Instead of averaging dimension scores, the study applies a Max Severity Principle so that a single legally decisive defect remains visible. The paper provides the evaluation protocol, taxonomy, analysis plan, and a results structure for reporting empirical findings. We argue that legal AI evaluation should move beyond aggregate accuracy toward clause-specific, failure-mode-driven, and risk-sensitive assessment.

</details>


### [240] [Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22131)
*Sicheng Xu,Hao Shi,Wei Zhang,Haoran Pang,Zhenyu Ming,Hao Wu,Zhongyi Huang,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相关性感知的结构化剪枝方法，将剪枝目标建模为带基数约束的二元二次规划以显式刻画单元间依赖，并用贪心交互算法与梯度自适应稀疏分配求解，在主流LLM上取得竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法多孤立评估可剪单元（如通道、注意力头）的重要性，隐含假设剪枝误差可加，但模型权重非正交、单元激活强相关使该独立性假设失效，导致性能下降。

Method: 将剪枝目标表述为带基数约束的二元二次规划，显式建模重构误差中的跨单元依赖；因该问题是NP-hard难以精确求解，提出基于依赖感知边际成本的贪心交互算法进行单元选择；并引入基于梯度的策略，在全模型范围内实现自适应逐层稀疏分配。

Result: 在主流LLM上的大量实验表明，融入相关性信息相比代表性结构化剪枝基线可获得具有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 显式建模单元间相关性可有效缓解独立性假设带来的性能损失，所提方法在结构化剪枝中实现了较好的精度与效率平衡。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for reducing the substantial inference costs of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining hardware efficiency. Many existing methods assess the importance of prunable units (e.g., channels or heads) in isolation, implicitly assuming that pruning errors are additive. This independence assumption is often invalidated by the non-orthogonality of model weights and strong correlations between unit activations, potentially leading to performance degradation. To address this, we propose a Correlation-Aware Structured Pruning method. We formulate the pruning objective as a cardinality-constrained binary quadratic program that explicitly models cross-unit dependencies in the reconstruction error. Since this binary quadratic program is NP-hard and difficult to solve exactly, we develop a greedy interaction algorithm based on dependency-aware marginal costs to optimize unit selection. Furthermore, we incorporate a gradient-based strategy to achieve adaptive layer-wise sparsity allocation across the entire model. Extensive experiments on mainstream LLMs demonstrate that incorporating correlation information yields competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to representative structured pruning baselines.

</details>


### [241] [Observational Equivalence of LLM and Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22133)
*Kentaro Nakamura,Jing Ling Tan,George Yean*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过复现14项政治学研究中的文本分类任务，证明LLM与人类专家在标注质量上观测等价，其分歧主要源于文本与编码规则的歧义，而非编码者类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于LLM能否替代人类进行文本标注存在争议，传统观点倾向于认为人类编码质量更高。作者希望检验这一假设是否有实证依据。

Method: 复现14项同行评审政治学研究的文本分类任务，让10个LLM、3位人类专家和165名众包工作者使用相同编码手册独立标注相同文本，并进行专家间与专家-LLM间一致性比较、规则澄清实验等分析。

Result: 近期LLM与专家的致率与专家彼此之间的致率相当；当LLM与专家分歧时，专家之间也更易分歧；澄清编码规则可同时降低专家与能力足够强的LLM之间的分歧。

Conclusion: 仅凭标注质量不足以偏好人类编码，LLM在速度与成本上具明显优势。文本标注的核心挑战已从"人机选择"转向"降低规则歧义并处理残余歧义"。为此作者提出利用LLM间分歧识别困难案例、优化编码手册，并为无法唯一标注的文本开发歧义感知的下游推断界限。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that LLM and human coding are observationally equivalent in terms of annotation quality: recent LLMs agree with expert coders at rates comparable to those observed among experts themselves. We demonstrate this through replications of text-classification tasks from 14 peer-reviewed political science studies, in which ten LLMs, three human experts, and 165 crowdsourced workers independently classify the same texts using identical codebooks. We find that this equivalence is driven by ambiguity in the texts and coding rules. When LLMs disagree with experts, experts are also more likely to disagree with one another, and clarifying coding rules reduces disagreement among both experts and sufficiently capable LLMs. Thus, there is little empirical basis for preferring human coding on the basis of annotation quality alone, while LLMs offer substantial advantages in speed and cost. We therefore argue that the central challenge of text annotation is no longer choosing between human and machine coders, but developing coding rules that minimize ambiguity and accounting for the ambiguity that remains. To this end, we propose using disagreement across LLMs to identify difficult cases and refine codebooks, and we develop ambiguity-aware bounds for downstream inference when a unique annotation cannot be defined for every text.

</details>


### [242] [Read-Best Is Not Steer-Best: A Probing--Steering Layer Dissociation in Omni-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22135)
*Yibo Wang,Jisheng Dang,Bimei Wang,Yitao Wu,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Jizhao Liu,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对"探针准确率最高的层也是最适合干预的层"这一假设进行因果检验，发现该假设在全模态大模型中不成立，提出"探测-干预层分离"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大模型将文本、音频、图像整合到共享残差流中，情绪等概念可以被线性解码并通过激活引导进行因果修改。然而领域内普遍假设探测准确率最高的层也是最佳干预层，并据此选择注入层，这一假设鲜有被检验。

Method: 在三个独立开发的全模态模型上，以情绪为受控测试平台，测量文本、音频、图像输入下逐层的可读性与可干预性。采用配对随机方向对照检验因果差距，并通过logit-lens分析前向过程。

Result: 探测最佳层在不同架构间差异很大，而干预有效层一致落在归一化深度的中后段狭窄区间内。配对随机方向对照显示约26倍的因果差距，排除了随机扰动与方向质量作为解释。Logit-lens揭示三阶段前向过程：因果把手、探测饱和、词表承诺。此外识别出按效价与唤醒度组织的跨模态情绪子空间，其中"joy"在各模型间作为稳定锚点。

Conclusion: 探测准确率是选择干预层的糟糕启发式，建议采用跨架构的中后层选择标准。干预效果取决于表征可读性与下游可塑性两个因素。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models integrate text, audio, and image signals into a shared residual stream, where concepts such as emotion can be linearly decoded and causally modified by activation steering. A common but rarely tested assumption is that the layer with the highest probing accuracy is also the best layer for steering, so injection layers are often selected by probe performance. We provide the first causal test of this assumption across three independently developed omni-modal models and find that it fails. Reading and intervention rely on different layers, a phenomenon we call the probing-steering layer dissociation. Using emotion as a controlled testbed, we measure layer-wise readability and steerability across text, audio, and image inputs. Probe-best layers vary widely across architectures, while steering-effective layers consistently fall within a narrow mid-to-late range of normalized depth. Paired random-direction controls show an approximately 26-fold causal gap, ruling out random perturbation and direction quality as explanations. Logit-lens analysis reveals a staged forward process: causal handle, probing saturation, and vocabulary commitment, and motivates a two-factor account in which steering effectiveness depends on both representational readability and downstream plasticity. These results show that probing accuracy is a poor heuristic for selecting intervention layers and suggest a cross-architecture mid-to-late selection criterion. We also identify a cross-modal emotion subspace organized by valence and arousal, with joy acting as a stable anchor across models. Code and data: https://github.com/YiboWang2002/Read-Best-Is-Not-Steer-Best.

</details>


### [243] [DiFA: Dual Evidence Fusion and Aggregation for Token-Level Text Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22136)
*Yanyu Qian,Pengcheng Weng,Yue Tan,Enguang Zuo,Yu Zheng,Yixin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiFA 框架用于 token 级文本异常检测，通过形式结构与语义双视角生成互补异常分数，并用校准融合与多元聚合机制实现更优检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本异常检测方法多局限于文档级检测，难以定位有害短语或支持针对性预防。新兴的 token 级检测方法主要依赖表示空间距离度量，忽略了不同异常线索在捕捉多样异常模式中的互补作用。

Method: 提出 DiFA（Dual-evidence with adaptive Fusion and Aggregation）框架：1）从形式-结构视角和语义视角分别导出异常分数，捕捉可见结构异常与上下文不一致；2）引入校准与融合机制，自适应平衡两个数值尺度不同的视角；3）设计多元聚合方法，从多角度汇总 token 级分数以获得有判别力的文档级分数，避免稀有异常 token 被稀释。

Result: 在多个文本异常检测基准上的大量实验表明，DiFA 持续取得最优性能，同时保持较强的效率、鲁棒性和可解释性。代码与脚本已开源。

Conclusion: DiFA 通过双证据互补与自适应融合聚合，有效解决了 token 级文本异常检测中线索单一与分数汇总不足的问题，具有良好的性能与实用价值。

Abstract: Text anomaly detection, the task of identifying text instances that deviate from normal language patterns, is crucial for language-driven applications. However, most existing methods can only perform document-level anomaly detection, making it hard to locate harmful phrases or support targeted prevention. Recently, there has been an emerging trend toward token-level text anomaly detection, which aims to address the above limitation by identifying anomalous words or fragments within a document. Nevertheless, one representative method mainly relies on representation-space distance measurement, neglecting the complementary roles of different anomaly cues in capturing diverse abnormal patterns. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Dual-evidence framework with adaptive Fusion and Aggregation (DiFA) for token-level anomaly detection. DiFA derives anomaly scores from form-structural and semantic views to capture visible structural abnormality and contextual inconsistency, respectively, thereby providing complementary evidence for identifying diverse anomalies. To combine these two scores with varying numerical scales, DiFA incorporates a calibration and fusion mechanism to adaptively balance the two views. Moreover, to obtain a discriminative document-level score, a multivariate aggregation method is designed to summarize token-level anomaly scores from multiple perspectives, preventing rare anomalous tokens from being diluted. Extensive experiments across various text anomaly detection benchmarks demonstrate that DiFA consistently achieves top performance while maintaining strong efficiency, robustness, and interpretability. The code and scripts are available at: https://github.com/qyy11-com/DiFA.

</details>
